It's so clear, I actually answer them out loud before I realize... before I realize no one else in the room heard it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other experiences where you believed something was happening or true that others don't seem to notice or agree with?\n\nPatient: I... no. I don't think so. Just the talking. I don't really know what else you mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that certain people are specifically targeting you or plotting against you in some way?\n\nPatient: I... no. Not really. I just... That's all.\n\nDoctor: Have there been times when you believed something strongly, like something was definitely going to happen or that someone definitely meant something, but it turned out not to be true?\n\nPatient: I... no. Not really. I don't make things up in my head. I usually know what's going on. I told you, it's... it's just the talking. Nothing else.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like the thoughts or things you hear are being placed inside your mind by someone else, or that they don't belong to you?\n\nPatient: No. I... my thoughts are my own.
